[31m+     signIn: (email: string, password: string, role?: string) => Promise<{ error: any }>;
[39m
[31m+     signInWithGoogle: () => Promise<{ error: any }>;
[39m
[31m+     signOut: () => Promise<void>;
[39m
[31m+     refreshUser: () => Promise<void>;
[39m
[31m+ }
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+ const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined);
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+ const VITE_API_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || '/api';
[39m
[31m+ const API_BASE = VITE_API_URL.endsWith('/api') ? VITE_API_URL : `${VITE_API_URL.replace(/\/$/, '')}/api`;
[39m
[31m+ const API_URL = `${API_BASE}/auth`;
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+ export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
[39m
[31m+     const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
[39m
[31m+     const [userProfile, setUserProfile] = useState<UserProfile | null>(null);
[39m
[31m+     const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+     // Initial load: Check if token exists
[39m
[31m+     useEffect(() => {
[39m
[31m+         const syncAuth = () => {
[39m
[31m+             const storedUser = localStorage.getItem('user');
[39m
[31m+             if (storedUser) {
[39m
[31m+                 try {
[39m
[31m+                     const parsedUser = JSON.parse(storedUser);
[39m
[31m+                     setUser(parsedUser);
[39m
[31m+                     setUserProfile({
[39m
[31m+                         uid: parsedUser._id,
[39m
[31m+                         email: parsedUser.email,
[39m
[31m+                         displayName: parsedUser.displayName,
[39m
[31m+                         role: parsedUser.role,
[39m
[31m+                         streak: 0,
[39m
[31m+                         lastActiveDate: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
[39m
[31m+                         createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
[39m
[31m+                     });
[39m
[31m+                 } catch (e) {
[39m
[31m+                     localStorage.removeItem('user');
[39m
[31m+                     setUser(null);
[39m
[31m+                     setUserProfile(null);
[39m
[31m+                 }
[39m
[31m+             } else {
[39m
[31m+                 setUser(null);
[39m
[31m+                 setUserProfile(null);
[39m
[31m+             }
[39m
[31m+             setLoading(false);
[39m
[31m+         };
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+         syncAuth();
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+         // Listen for changes from other tabs
[39m
[31m+         window.addEventListener('storage', (e) => {
[39m
[31m+             if (e.key === 'user') {
[39m
[31m+                 syncAuth();
[39m
[31m+             }
[39m
[31m+         });
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+         return () => window.removeEventListener('storage', syncAuth);
[39m
[31m+     }, []);
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+     const signUp = async (email: string, password: string, displayName: string, role: string) => {
[39m
[31m+         try {
[39m
[31m+             const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}/register`, {
[39m
[31m+                 method: 'POST',
[39m
[31m+                 headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
[39m
[31m+                 body: JSON.stringify({ email, password, displayName, role }),
[39m
[31m+             });
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+             const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({ message: 'Invalid server response' }));
[39m
[31m+             if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.message || 'Signup failed');
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+             localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(data));
[39m
[31m+             setUser(data);
[39m
[31m+             setUserProfile({
[39m
[31m+                 uid: data._id,
[39m
[31m+                 email: data.email,
[39m
[31m+                 displayName: data.displayName,
[39m
[31m+                 role: data.role,
[39m
[31m+                 streak: 0,
[39m
[31m+                 lastActiveDate: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
[39m
[31m+                 createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
[39m
[31m+             });
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+             return { error: null };
[39m
[31m+         } catch (error: any) {
[39m
[31m+             console.error('Signup error:', error);
[39m
[31m+             return { error };
[39m
[31m+         }
[39m
[31m+     };
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+     const signIn = async (email: string, password: string, role?: string) => {
[39m
[31m+         try {
[39m
[31m+             const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}/login`, {
[39m
[31m+                 method: 'POST',
[39m
[31m+                 headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
[39m
[31m+                 body: JSON.stringify({ email, password, role }),
[39m
[31m+             });
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+             const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({ message: 'Invalid server response' }));
[39m
[31m+             if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.message || 'Login failed');
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+             localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(data));
[39m
[31m+             setUser(data);
[39m
[31m+             setUserProfile({
[39m
[31m+                 uid: data._id,
[39m
[31m+                 email: data.email,
[39m
[31m+                 displayName: data.displayName,
[39m
[31m+                 role: data.role,
[39m
[31m+                 streak: 0,
[39m
[31m+                 lastActiveDate: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
[39m
[31m+                 createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
[39m
[31m+             });
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+             return { error: null };
[39m
[31m+         } catch (error: any) {
[39m
[31m+             console.error('Signin error:', error);
[39m
[31m+             return { error };
[39m
[31m+         }
[39m
[31m+     };
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+     const signOut = async () => {
[39m
[31m+         localStorage.removeItem('user');
[39m
[31m+         setUser(null);
[39m
[31m+         setUserProfile(null);
[39m
[31m+     };
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+     const refreshUser = async () => {
[39m
[31m+         if (!user) return;
[39m
[31m+         try {
[39m
[31m+             const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/user/profile`, {
[39m
[31m+                 headers: {
[39m
[31m+                     'Authorization': `Bearer ${user.token}`
[39m
[31m+                 }
[39m
[31m+             });
[39m
[31m+             if (response.ok) {
[39m
[31m+                 const data = await response.json();
[39m
[31m+                 const updatedUser = { ...user, ...data };
[39m
[31m+                 localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(updatedUser));
[39m
[31m+                 setUser(updatedUser);
[39m
[31m+             }
[39m
[31m+         } catch (error) {
[39m
[31m+             console.error('Error refreshing user:', error);
[39m
[31m+         }
[39m
[31m+     };
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+     return (
[39m
[31m+         <AuthContext.Provider value={{ user, userProfile, loading, signUp, signIn, signOut, refreshUser, signInWithGoogle: async () => ({ error: 'Not implemented' }) }}>
[39m
[31m+             {children}
[39m
[31m+         </AuthContext.Provider>
[39m
[31m+     );
[39m
[31m+ }

[31m+         throw new Error('useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider');
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     return context;

[31m+             const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}/register`, {

[31m+                 body: JSON.stringify({ email, password, displayName, role }),
[39m
[31m+             });
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+             const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({ message: 'Invalid server response' }));
[39m
[31m+             if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.message || 'Signup failed');
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+             localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(data));
[39m

[39m
[31m+                 uid: data._id,
[39m
[31m+                 email: data.email,
[39m
[31m+                 displayName: data.displayName,
[39m
[31m+                 role: data.role,
[39m
[31m+                 streak: 0,
[39m
[31m+                 lastActiveDate: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
[39m
[31m+                 createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
[39m
[31m+             });
[39m
[31m+

[31m+             return { error: null };

[31m+         } catch (error: any) {
[39m
[31m+             console.error('Signup error:', error);

[31m+         }

[31m+     };

[31m+

[39m

[39m
[31m+                 method: 'POST',
[39m
[31m+                 headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },

[39m
[31m+             });
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+             const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({ message: 'Invalid server response' }));
[39m
[31m+             if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.message || 'Login failed');
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+             localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(data));
[39m
[31m+             setUser(data);
[39m
[31m+             setUserProfile({
[39m
[31m+                 uid: data._id,
[39m
[31m+                 email: data.email,
[39m
[31m+                 displayName: data.displayName,
[39m
[31m+                 role: data.role,
[39m
[31m+                 streak: 0,
[39m

[31m+                 createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
[39m

[39m
[31m+         }

[31m+     };

[31m+     const signOut = async () => {
[39m
[31m+         localStorage.removeItem('user');

[31m+         setUser(null);
[39m
[31m+         setUserProfile(null);
[39m
[31m+     };
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+     const refreshUser = async () => {
[39m
[31m+         if (!user) return;
[39m
[31m+         try {
[39m
[31m+             const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/user/profile`, {
[39m
[31m+                 headers: {
[39m
[31m+                     'Authorization': `Bearer ${user.token}`
[39m
[31m+                 }
[39m
[31m+             });
[39m
[31m+             if (response.ok) {
[39m
[31m+                 const data = await response.json();
[39m
[31m+                 const updatedUser = { ...user, ...data };
[39m
[31m+                 localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(updatedUser));
[39m
[31m+                 setUser(updatedUser);
[39m
[31m+             }
[39m
[31m+         } catch (error) {
[39m
[31m+             console.error('Error refreshing user:', error);
[39m
[31m+         }
[39m
[31m+     };
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+     return (
[39m
[31m+         <AuthContext.Provider value={{ user, userProfile, loading, signUp, signIn, signOut, refreshUser, signInWithGoogle: async () => ({ error: 'Not implemented' }) }}>
[39m
[31m+             {children}
[39m
[31m+         </AuthContext.Provider>
[39m
[31m+     );
[39m
[31m+ }
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+ export function useAuth() {
[39m
[31m+     const context = useContext(AuthContext);
[39m
[31m+     if (context === undefined) {
[39m
[31m+         throw new Error('useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider');
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     return context;
[39m
[31m+ }

[31m+   const selectAnswer = (answer: 'A' | 'B' | 'C' | 'D') => {
[39m
[31m+     if (showResult) return;
[39m
[31m+     setSelectedAnswer(answer);
[39m
[31m+   };
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+   const submitAnswer = () => {
[39m
[31m+     if (!selectedAnswer) return;
[39m
[31m+     const q = questions[currentIndex];
[39m
[31m+     const isCorrect = selectedAnswer === q.correct_answer;
[39m
[31m+     setAnswers((prev) => [...prev, { questionIndex: currentIndex, selected: selectedAnswer, isCorrect }]);
[39m
[31m+     setShowResult(true);
[39m
[31m+   };
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+   const nextQuestion = async () => {
[39m
[31m+     if (currentIndex + 1 >= questions.length) {
[39m
[31m+       // Complete ΓÇö save score (answers already includes the last answer from submitAnswer)
[39m
[31m+       const finalAnswers = answers;
[39m
[31m+       const total = questions.length;
[39m
[31m+       const finalScore = finalAnswers.filter((a) => a.isCorrect).length;
[39m
[31m+       const percentage = Math.round((finalScore / total) * 100);
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+       if (user) {
[39m
[31m+         try {
[39m
[31m+           await saveScoreRecord({
[39m
[31m+             quizId: `adaptive-${subject}-${Date.now()}`,
[39m
[31m+             quizTitle: `Adaptive Quiz: ${subject}`,
[39m
[31m+             score: finalScore,
[39m
[31m+             total,
[39m
[31m+             percentage,
[39m
[31m+             completedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
[39m
[31m+             subject,
[39m
[31m+           });
[39m
[31m+           await evaluateBadges(user._id, records, streak);
[39m
[31m+         } catch (err) {
[39m
[31m+           console.error('Failed to save score or evaluate badges:', err);
[39m
[31m+         }
[39m
[31m+       }
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+       setPageState('results');
[39m
[31m+     } else {
[39m
[31m+       setCurrentIndex((i) => i + 1);
[39m
[31m+       setSelectedAnswer(null);
[39m
[31m+       setShowResult(false);
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+   };
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+   // ΓöÇΓöÇ Results helpers ΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇ
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+   const totalScore = answers.filter((a) => a.isCorrect).length;
[39m
[31m+   const totalQuestions = questions.length;
[39m
[31m+   const percentage = totalQuestions > 0 ? Math.round((totalScore / totalQuestions) * 100) : 0;
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+   // Top 2 incorrect topics (question text of wrong answers)
[39m
[31m+   const incorrectTopics = answers
[39m
[31m+     .filter((a) => !a.isCorrect)
[39m
[31m+     .map((a) => questions[a.questionIndex]?.question_text ?? '')
[39m
[31m+     .filter(Boolean);
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+   const top2Topics = incorrectTopics.slice(0, 2);
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+   // Find matching note for a topic (by subject match)
[39m
[31m+   const findNoteForTopic = (_topic: string): Note | undefined => {
[39m
[31m+     const publishedNotes = notes.filter((n) => n.published);
[39m
[31m+     return publishedNotes.find(
[39m
[31m+       (n) => n.subject.toLowerCase() === subject.toLowerCase()
[39m
[31m+     );
[39m
[31m+   };
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+   // ΓöÇΓöÇ Render ΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇ
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+   const cardStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
[39m
[31m+     background: '#FFFFFF',
[39m
[31m+     borderRadius: 24,
[39m
[31m+     padding: 32,
[39m
[31m+     border: '1px solid #F1F5F9',
[39m
[31m+     boxShadow: '0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.06)',
[39m
[31m+   };
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+   return (
[39m
[31m+     <div style={{ minHeight: '100vh', background: '#F5F5F5', fontFamily: "'Inter', sans-serif" }}>
[39m
[31m+       <Helmet>
[39m
[31m+         <title>Adaptive Quiz ΓÇö Quizly</title>
[39m
[31m+         <meta name="description" content="AI-powered adaptive quiz tailored to your performance." />
[39m
[31m+       </Helmet>

[31m+       <StudentNavbar />
[39m

[39m
[31m+       <div style={{ maxWidth: 720, margin: '0 auto', padding: '40px 24px' }}>
[39m
[31m+         {/* Breadcrumb */}
[39m
[31m+         <Link
[39m
[31m+           to="/student/library"
[39m
[31m+           style={{
[39m
[31m+             display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6,
[39m
[31m+             color: '#6B7280', textDecoration: 'none', fontSize: 14,
[39m
[31m+             fontWeight: 500, marginBottom: 28,
[39m
[31m+           }}
[39m
[31m+         >
[39m
[31m+           <ArrowLeft size={15} />
[39m
[31m+           Back to Library
[39m
[31m+         </Link>
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+         {/* ΓöÇΓöÇ Loading ΓöÇΓöÇ */}
[39m
[31m+         {pageState === 'loading' && (
[39m
[31m+           <div style={{ ...cardStyle, textAlign: 'center', padding: '60px 32px' }}>
[39m
[31m+             <div style={{
[39m
[31m+               width: 56, height: 56, borderRadius: '50%',
[39m
[31m+               border: '4px solid #F1F5F9', borderTopColor: '#FF5C1A',
[39m
[31m+               animation: 'spin 0.8s linear infinite', margin: '0 auto 20px',
[39m
[31m+             }} />
[39m
[31m+             <p style={{ fontSize: 17, fontWeight: 700, color: '#111827', margin: '0 0 8px' }}>
[39m
[31m+               Generating your adaptive quizΓÇª
[39m
[31m+             </p>
[39m
[31m+             <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: '#6B7280', margin: 0 }}>
[39m
[31m+               Analysing your performance and crafting personalised questions.

[31m+             </p>

[31m+             <style>{`@keyframes spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg); } }`}</style>
[39m
[31m+           </div>
[39m
[31m+         )}
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+         {/* ΓöÇΓöÇ Error ΓöÇΓöÇ */}
[39m
[31m+         {pageState === 'error' && (
[39m
[31m+           <div style={{ ...cardStyle, textAlign: 'center', padding: '60px 32px' }}>
[39m

[39m
[31m+               <button
[39m
[31m+                 onClick={handleRetry}
[39m
[31m+                 style={{
[39m

[31m+               <Link
[39m
[31m+                 to="/student"

[31m+                   padding: '10px 20px', borderRadius: 10,

[31m+                   background: '#F3F4F6', color: '#374151',

[39m

[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+         {/* ΓöÇΓöÇ Quiz in progress ΓöÇΓöÇ */}
[39m
[31m+         {pageState === 'quiz' && questions.length > 0 && (
[39m
[31m+           <div style={{
[39m
[31m+             background: '#FFFFFF',
[39m
[31m+             borderRadius: '24px',

[39m
[31m+               <div style={{
[39m
[31m+                 width: 40, height: 40, borderRadius: 12, background: 'rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.1)',

[39m

[39m
[31m+                 <Brain size={20} color="#6366F1" />
[39m
[31m+               </div>
[39m
[31m+               <div>
[39m
[31m+                 <span style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700, color: '#6366F1', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.05em' }}>
[39m
[31m+                   AI-Powered Adaptive Quiz
[39m
[31m+                 </span>

[39m

[39m

[39m
[31m+               <motion.div
[39m
[31m+                 initial={{ width: 0 }}
[39m

[39m

[39m
[31m+                   height: '100%', borderRadius: 10,
[39m

[39m

[39m

[39m

[39m
[31m+                 background: questions[currentIndex].difficulty === 'easy' ? '#DCFCE7' :
[39m
[31m+                   questions[currentIndex].difficulty === 'medium' ? '#FEF9C3' : '#FEE2E2',
[39m
[31m+                 color: questions[currentIndex].difficulty === 'easy' ? '#16A34A' :
[39m
[31m+                   questions[currentIndex].difficulty === 'medium' ? '#CA8A04' : '#DC2626',
[39m

[39m
[31m+                 {questions[currentIndex].difficulty} Level
[39m
[31m+               </span>

[39m
[31m+                 {questions[currentIndex].question_text}
[39m
[31m+               </h2>
[39m
[31m+             </div>
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+             {/* Options */}
[39m
[31m+             <div className="grid grid-2 gap-md" style={{ marginBottom: 32 }}>
[39m
[31m+               {(['A', 'B', 'C', 'D'] as const).map((letter) => {
[39m
[31m+                 const optionKey = `option_${letter.toLowerCase()}` as keyof GeneratedQuestion;
[39m
[31m+                 const optionText = questions[currentIndex][optionKey] as string;
[39m
[31m+                 const isSelected = selectedAnswer === letter;
[39m
[31m+                 const isCorrect = questions[currentIndex].correct_answer === letter;
[39m
[31m+                 const showCorrectness = showResult;
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+                 return (
[39m
[31m+                   <motion.button
[39m
[31m+                     key={letter}
[39m
[31m+                     whileHover={!showResult ? { scale: 1.02 } : {}}
[39m
[31m+                     whileTap={!showResult ? { scale: 0.98 } : {}}
[39m
[31m+                     onClick={() => selectAnswer(letter)}
[39m
[31m+                     disabled={showResult}
[39m
[31m+                     className={`answer-btn answer-${letter.toLowerCase()} ${isSelected ? 'selected' : ''} ${showCorrectness && isCorrect ? 'correct' : ''
[39m
[31m+                       } ${showCorrectness && isSelected && !isCorrect ? 'incorrect' : ''}`}
[39m
[31m+                     style={{
[39m
[31m+                       opacity: showCorrectness && !isCorrect && !isSelected ? 0.4 : 1,
[39m
[31m+                       pointerEvents: showResult ? 'none' : 'auto',
[39m
[31m+                       minHeight: '70px',
[39m
[31m+                       padding: '1rem 1.25rem',
[39m
[31m+                       fontSize: '1rem',
[39m
[31m+                       boxShadow: isSelected ? '0 0 0 3px rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.4)' : 'none'
[39m
[31m+                     }}
[39m
[31m+                   >
[39m
[31m+                     <div style={{
[39m
[31m+                       width: '28px', height: '28px',
[39m

[39m
[31m+                       borderRadius: '8px',
[39m
[31m+                       display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
[39m

[39m
[31m+                     }}>
[39m

[39m

[39m

[39m
[31m+                       {optionText}
[39m

[39m

[39m

[39m

[39m

[39m

[39m
[31m+                   style={{ minWidth: '220px' }}
[39m

[39m

[39m

[39m

[39m

[39m

[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+ // ΓöÇΓöÇ ResultsSummary sub-component ΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇ
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+ interface ResultsSummaryProps {
[39m
[31m+   questions: GeneratedQuestion[];
[39m
[31m+   answers: QuizAnswer[];
[39m
[31m+   totalScore: number;
[39m
[31m+   totalQuestions: number;
[39m
[31m+   percentage: number;
[39m
[31m+   subject: string;
[39m
[31m+   top2Topics: string[];
[39m
[31m+   findNoteForTopic: (topic: string) => Note | undefined;
[39m
[31m+   onRetry: () => void;
[39m
[31m+ }
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+ function ResultsSummary({
[39m
[31m+   questions,
[39m
[31m+   answers,
[39m
[31m+   totalScore,
[39m
[31m+   totalQuestions,
[39m
[31m+   percentage,
[39m
[31m+   subject,
[39m
[31m+   top2Topics,
[39m
[31m+   findNoteForTopic,
[39m
[31m+   onRetry,
[39m
[31m+ }: ResultsSummaryProps) {
[39m
[31m+   const cardStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
[39m
[31m+     background: '#FFFFFF',
[39m
[31m+     borderRadius: 24,
[39m

[31m+   // ΓöÇΓöÇ Results helpers ΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇ

[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+   const totalScore = answers.filter((a) => a.isCorrect).length;
[39m
[31m+   const totalQuestions = questions.length;
[39m
[31m+   const percentage = totalQuestions > 0 ? Math.round((totalScore / totalQuestions) * 100) : 0;
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+   // Top 2 incorrect topics (question text of wrong answers)
[39m
[31m+   const incorrectTopics = answers
[39m
[31m+     .filter((a) => !a.isCorrect)

[31m+     .map((a) => questions[a.questionIndex]?.question_text ?? '')

[31m+   const top2Topics = incorrectTopics.slice(0, 2);

[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+   // Find matching note for a topic (by subject match)
[39m
[31m+   const findNoteForTopic = (_topic: string): Note | undefined => {
[39m
[31m+     const publishedNotes = notes.filter((n) => n.published);
[39m
[31m+     return publishedNotes.find(
[39m
[31m+       (n) => n.subject.toLowerCase() === subject.toLowerCase()
[39m
[31m+     );
[39m
[31m+   };

[31m+   // ΓöÇΓöÇ Render ΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇ

[39m

[39m

[39m
[31m+ // ΓöÇΓöÇ ResultsSummary sub-component ΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇ
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+ interface ResultsSummaryProps {
[39m
[31m+   questions: GeneratedQuestion[];
[39m
[31m+   answers: QuizAnswer[];
[39m
[31m+   totalScore: number;
[39m
[31m+   totalQuestions: number;
[39m
[31m+   percentage: number;
[39m
[31m+   subject: string;
[39m
[31m+   top2Topics: string[];
[39m
[31m+   findNoteForTopic: (topic: string) => Note | undefined;
[39m
[31m+   onRetry: () => void;
[39m
[31m+ }
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+ function ResultsSummary({
[39m
[31m+   questions,
[39m
[31m+   answers,
[39m
[31m+   totalScore,
[39m
[31m+   totalQuestions,
[39m
[31m+   percentage,
[39m
[31m+   subject,
[39m
[31m+   top2Topics,
[39m
[31m+   findNoteForTopic,
[39m
[31m+   onRetry,
[39m
[31m+ }: ResultsSummaryProps) {
[39m
[31m+   const cardStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
[39m
[31m+     background: '#FFFFFF',
[39m
[31m+     borderRadius: 24,
[39m
[31m+     padding: 32,
[39m
[31m+     border: '1px solid #F1F5F9',
[39m
[31m+     boxShadow: '0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.06)',
[39m
[31m+     marginBottom: 20,
[39m
[31m+   };
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+   const scoreColor = percentage >= 80 ? '#16A34A' : percentage >= 60 ? '#CA8A04' : '#DC2626';
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+   return (
[39m
[31m+     <>
[39m
[31m+       {/* Score card */}
[39m
[31m+       <div style={{ ...cardStyle, textAlign: 'center' }}>
[39m
[31m+         <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 10, marginBottom: 16 }}>
[39m
[31m+           <Brain size={22} color="#6366F1" />
[39m
[31m+           <span style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, color: '#6366F1' }}>
[39m
[31m+             Adaptive Quiz Complete ΓÇö {subject}
[39m
[31m+           </span>
[39m
[31m+         </div>
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+         <div style={{
[39m
[31m+           fontSize: 64, fontWeight: 900, color: scoreColor,
[39m
[31m+           lineHeight: 1, marginBottom: 8,
[39m
[31m+         }}>
[39m
[31m+           {percentage}%
[39m
[31m+         </div>

[39m

[39m
[31m+         </p>
[39m
[31m+         <p style={{ fontSize: 14, color: '#9CA3AF', margin: 0 }}>
[39m
[31m+           {percentage >= 80 ? 'Excellent work!' : percentage >= 60 ? 'Good effort!' : 'Keep going!'}
[39m
[31m+         </p>
[39m
[31m+       </div>
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+       {/* Motivational message + Retry for < 60% */}
[39m
[31m+       {percentage < 60 && (
[39m
[31m+         <div style={{
[39m
[31m+           ...cardStyle,
[39m
[31m+           background: '#FFF7ED',
[39m
[31m+           border: '1px solid #FED7AA',
[39m
[31m+           display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between',
[39m
[31m+           flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 16,
[39m

[31m+ // Pure filter function (exported for testing)
[39m
[31m+ // eslint-disable-next-line react-refresh/only-export-components
[39m
[31m+ export function filterLibraryQuizzes(
[39m
[31m+     quizzes: LibraryQuiz[],
[39m
[31m+     query: string,
[39m
[31m+     subject: string
[39m
[31m+ ): LibraryQuiz[] {
[39m
[31m+     let result = quizzes;
[39m
[31m+     if (query.trim()) {
[39m
[31m+         const lower = query.toLowerCase();
[39m
[31m+         result = result.filter(
[39m
[31m+             q => q.title.toLowerCase().includes(lower) || q.subject.toLowerCase().includes(lower)
[39m
[31m+         );
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     if (subject !== 'all') {
[39m
[31m+         result = result.filter(q => q.subject === subject);
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     return result;
[39m
[31m+ }

[39m
[31m+ function sortQuizzes(quizzes: LibraryQuiz[], sort: string): LibraryQuiz[] {
[39m
[31m+     return [...quizzes].sort((a, b) => {
[39m
[31m+         if (sort === 'plays') return (b.plays ?? 0) - (a.plays ?? 0);
[39m
[31m+         if (sort === 'rating') return (b.rating ?? 0) - (a.rating ?? 0);
[39m
[31m+         return 0;
[39m
[31m+     });
[39m
[31m+ }

[39m
[31m+     async function handleNoteSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
[39m
[31m+         e.preventDefault();
[39m
[31m+         const errors: { title?: string; content?: string } = {};
[39m
[31m+         if (!noteForm.title.trim()) errors.title = 'Title is required';
[39m
[31m+         if (!noteForm.content.trim()) errors.content = 'Content is required';
[39m
[31m+         setNoteFormErrors(errors);
[39m
[31m+         if (Object.keys(errors).length > 0) return;
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+         setNoteSubmitting(true);
[39m
[31m+         try {
[39m
[31m+             await apiFetch('/api/notes', {
[39m
[31m+                 method: 'POST',
[39m
[31m+                 body: {
[39m
[31m+                     title: noteForm.title.trim(),
[39m
[31m+                     subject: noteForm.subject.trim(),
[39m
[31m+                     content: noteForm.content.trim(),
[39m
[31m+                     linkedQuizId: noteForm.linkedQuizId.trim() || null,
[39m
[31m+                 }
[39m
[31m+             });
[39m
[31m+             setNoteForm(EMPTY_NOTE_FORM);
[39m
[31m+             setNoteFormErrors({});
[39m
[31m+             setShowNoteForm(false);
[39m
[31m+             refresh();
[39m
[31m+         } catch (err) {
[39m
[31m+             console.error('Failed to save note:', err);
[39m
[31m+         } finally {
[39m
[31m+             setNoteSubmitting(false);
[39m
[31m+         }
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+     async function handlePublishToggle(note: Note) {
[39m
[31m+         try {
[39m
[31m+             await apiFetch(`/api/notes/${note.id || (note as any)._id}`, {
[39m
[31m+                 method: 'PATCH',
[39m
[31m+                 body: { published: !note.published }
[39m
[31m+             });
[39m
[31m+             refresh();
[39m
[31m+         } catch (err) {
[39m
[31m+             console.error('Failed to toggle publish state:', err);
[39m
[31m+         }
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+     async function handleDeleteNote(id: string) {
[39m
[31m+         if (!window.confirm('Are you sure you want to delete this note?')) return;
[39m
[31m+         try {
[39m
[31m+             await apiFetch(`/api/notes/${id}`, {
[39m
[31m+                 method: 'DELETE'
[39m
[31m+             });
[39m
[31m+             refresh();
[39m
[31m+         } catch (err) {
[39m
[31m+             console.error('Failed to delete note:', err);
[39m
[31m+         }
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m
[31m+     return (
[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex min-h-screen bg-[#F5F5F5] overflow-x-hidden">
[39m
[31m+             <TeacherSidebar isOpen={isSidebarOpen} onClose={() => setIsSidebarOpen(false)} />
[39m
[31m+ 
[39m

[31m+ function friendlyError(code: string): string {
[39m
[31m+     if (code.includes('invalid-credential') || code.includes('wrong-password') || code.includes('user-not-found')) {
[39m
[31m+         return 'Incorrect email or password. Please try again.';
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     if (code.includes('too-many-requests')) {
[39m
[31m+         return 'Too many failed attempts. Please wait a moment and try again.';
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     if (code.includes('user-disabled')) {
[39m
[31m+         return 'This account has been disabled. Contact support.';
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     if (code.includes('network-request-failed')) {
[39m
[31m+         return 'Network error. Check your connection and try again.';
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     if (code.includes('popup-closed-by-user')) {
[39m
[31m+         return 'Sign-in popup was closed. Please try again.';
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     if (code.includes('not supported')) {
[39m
[31m+         return code;
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     return 'Something went wrong. Please try again.';
[39m
[31m+ }

[31m+     const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
[39m
[31m+         e.preventDefault();

[31m+         setError('');

[31m+         setSubmitting(true);

[31m+         try {
[39m
[31m+             localStorage.setItem('userRole', role);
[39m
[31m+             const { error: signInError } = await signIn(email, password, role);
[39m
[31m+             if (signInError) {
[39m
[31m+                 const code = (signInError as { code?: string }).code ?? signInError.message ?? '';
[39m
[31m+                 setError(friendlyError(code));
[39m
[31m+             }
[39m
[31m+         } catch (err: any) {
[39m
[31m+             setError(err.message || 'An error occurred during sign in');

[31m+         } finally {
[39m
[31m+             setSubmitting(false);
[39m
[31m+         }
[39m
[31m+     };

[31m+         setSubmitting(true);

[39m
[31m+ function friendlyError(code: string): string {
[39m
[31m+     if (code.includes('invalid-credential') || code.includes('wrong-password') || code.includes('user-not-found')) {
[39m
[31m+         return 'Incorrect email or password. Please try again.';
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     if (code.includes('too-many-requests')) {
[39m
[31m+         return 'Too many failed attempts. Please wait a moment and try again.';
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     if (code.includes('user-disabled')) {
[39m
[31m+         return 'This account has been disabled. Contact support.';
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     if (code.includes('network-request-failed')) {
[39m
[31m+         return 'Network error. Check your connection and try again.';
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     if (code.includes('popup-closed-by-user')) {
[39m
[31m+         return 'Sign-in popup was closed. Please try again.';
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     if (code.includes('not supported')) {
[39m
[31m+         return code;
[39m
[31m+     }
[39m
[31m+     return 'Something went wrong. Please try again.';
[39m
[31m+ }
